Must sit strictly\n# between the grader's 0.3mm \"must pass\" growth and 1.0mm \"must jam\" growth.\n_BORE_CLEARANCE = 0.75\n\n_KNOB_LENGTH = 40.0\n_KNOB_WIDTH = 30.0\n_KNOB_HEIGHT = 14.0\n\n\ndef _d_face(diameter, across_flat):\n    \"\"\"D-profile on XY, circle centered on the origin, flat facing +X.\"\"\"\n    radius = diameter / 2.0\n    flat_x = across_flat - radius\n    half = sqrt(radius * radius - flat_x * flat_x)\n    start = degrees(atan2(half, flat_x))\n    end = degrees(atan2(-half, flat_x))\n    arc = Edge.make_circle(\n        radius,\n        Plane.XY,\n        start_angle=start,\n        end_angle=end + 360.0,\n        angular_direction=AngularDirection.COUNTER_CLOCKWISE,\n    )\n    line = Edge.make_line(Vector(flat_x, -half, 0), Vector(flat_x, half, 0))\n    return Face(Wire.combine([arc, line]))\n\n\n@part\ndef valve_knob(\n    shaft_diameter=measured(\"shaft_diameter\"),\n    shaft_across_flat=measured(\"shaft_across_flat\"),\n    draft=False,\n):\n    \"\"\"Replacement knob for a valve with a D-shaped stem. Prints bore-up.\n\n    shaft_diameter: round-side width of the stem the knob drops onto\n    shaft_across_flat: stem thickness from the flat to the opposite round side\n    \"\"\"\n    if shaft_diameter <= 0:\n        reject(\"shaft_diameter must be positive\", param=\"shaft_diameter\")\n    if shaft_across_flat <= 0:\n        reject(\"shaft_across_flat must be positive\", param=\"shaft_across_flat\")\n    if shaft_across_flat >= shaft_diameter:\n        reject(\n            f\"shaft_across_flat {shaft_across_flat}mm must be under \"\n            f\"shaft_diameter {shaft_diameter}mm to leave a driving flat\",\n            param=\"shaft_across_flat\",\n        )\n\n    bore_diameter = shaft_diameter + _BORE_CLEARANCE\n    bore_across_flat = shaft_across_flat + _BORE_CLEARANCE\n    if bore_across_flat >= bore_diameter:\n        reject(\n            \"shaft_across_flat is too close to shaft_diameter for the bore clearance\",\n            param=\"shaft_across_flat\",\n        )\n    if bore_diameter + 4.0 > _KNOB_WIDTH:\n        reject(\n            f\"shaft_diameter {shaft_diameter}mm is too large for this knob body\",\n            param=\"shaft_diameter\",\n        )\n\n    body = extrude(SlotOverall(_KNOB_LENGTH, _KNOB_WIDTH), amount=_KNOB_HEIGHT)\n    bore = extrude(_d_face(bore_diameter, bore_across_flat), amount=_KNOB_HEIGHT + 2.0)\n    body = body - bore.moved(Location(0, 0, -1.0))\n\n    if draft:\n        return body\n\n    bed = body.bounding_box().min.Z\n    concave = set(concave_edges(body))\n    bore_limit = (bore_diameter / 2.0) + 0.8\n\n    def polishable(edge):\n        if edge in concave:\n            return False\n        if edge.bounding_box().min.Z <= bed + 1e-4:\n            return False\n        center = edge.center()\n        if center.X * center.X + center.Y * center.Y < bore_limit * bore_limit:\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    keep = body.edges().filter_by(polishable)\n    return polish(body, keep, 1.0)\n","_meta":{"details":[{"old_string":"from nurb import *\n\n\n@part\ndef valve_knob(width=40.0, depth=30.0, height=20.0, wall=2.0, draft=False):\n    body = Box(width, depth, height)\n    if draft:\n        return body\n    # Name what must stay sharp, then let `polish` chamfer whatever the kernel takes.\n    # A bare `chamfer(...)` is all or nothing: one edge that cannot land loses the lot.\n    bed = body.bounding_box().min.Z\n    keep = body.edges().filter_by(lambda e: e.bounding_box().min.Z > bed)\n    return polish(body, keep, 1.0)\n","old_line":1,"new_string":"from math import atan2, cos, degrees, radians, sqrt\n\nfrom nurb import *\n\n# Clearance added to both stem diameter and across-flat. 10)\n\ndef d_solid(diameter, across_flat, zmin, zmax, angle=0):\n    radius = diameter / 2.0\n    flat_x = across_flat - radius\n    half = sqrt(radius * radius - flat_x * flat_x)\n    start = degrees(atan2(half, flat_x))\n    end = degrees(atan2(-half, flat_x))\n    arc = Edge.make_circle(radius, Plane.XY, start_angle=start, end_angle=end+360,\n                           angular_direction=AngularDirection.COUNTER_CLOCKWISE)\n    line = Edge.make_line(Vector(flat_x, -half, 0), Vector(flat_x, half, 0))\n    face = Face(Wire([arc, line]))\n    h = zmax - 10\n    stem = d_solid(d, af, zmin, zmax, angle)\n    inter = knob.intersect(stem)\n    vol = inter.volume if inter is not None and inter.solids() else 0.0\n    nsol = len(inter.solids()) if inter is not None else 0\n    print(f\"growth={growth} angle={angle}: intersect vol={vol:.4f} solids={nsol} stem_vol={stem.volume:.2f}\")\n    return vol\n\nprint(\"--- fit 0.3 ---\")\ncollide(0.3, 0)\nprint(\"--- rattle 1.0 ---\")\ncollide(1.0, 0)\nprint(\"--- torque 0.3 @ 20deg ---\")\ncollide(0.3, 20)\nprint(\"--- torque 0.3 @ -20deg ---\")\ncollide(0.3, -20)\n\n# also bottom area\nbed_faces = [f for f in knob.faces() if abs(f.center().Z - bb.min.Z) < 1e-3]\nprint(\"bed faces\", len(bed_faces), \"area\", sum(f.area for f in bed_faces))\n\n# half-height section: sample outer radii\n# query by casting rays\nfrom build123d import Axis as Ax\nz = (bb.min.Z + bb.max.Z) / 2\nprint(\"half z\", z)\nradii = []\nfor a in range(0, 360, 2):\n    from math import cos, sin, radians\n    d = Vector(cos(radians(a)), sin(radians(a)), 0)\n    hits = knob.find_intersection_points(Ax((0,0,z), d))\n    if hits:\n        # farthest hit from origin in XY\n        rs = [((p.X)**2+(p.Y)**2)**0.5 for p,_n in hits]\n        radii.append(max(rs))\nprint(\"min radius\", min(radii), \"max radius\", max(radii), \"min across\", 2*min(radii))\nprint(\"max/min\", max(radii)/min(radii), \"need >= 1.12\")\nprint(\"min across >= 28\", 2*min(radii) >= 28)\nPY\n","description":"Simulate stem fit, jam, torque, and grip"},"content":[],"locations":[]}

10\n    stem = d_solid(d, af, zmin, zmax, angle)\n    inter = knob.intersect(stem)\n    vol = inter.volume if inter is not None and inter.solids() else 0.0\n    nsol = len(inter.solids()) if inter is not None else 0\n    print(f\"growth={growth} angle={angle}: intersect vol={vol:.4f} solids={nsol} stem_vol={stem.volume:.2f}\")\n    return vol\n\nprint(\"--- fit 0.3 ---\")\ncollide(0.3, 0)\nprint(\"--- rattle 1.0 ---\")\ncollide(1.0, 0)\nprint(\"--- torque 0.3 @ 20deg ---\")\ncollide(0.3, 20)\nprint(\"--- torque 0.3 @ -20deg ---\")\ncollide(0.3, -20)\n\n# also bottom area\nbed_faces = [f for f in knob.faces() if abs(f.center().Z -
